# MAT Source Navigation

Generated by [the source navigation builder](../../scripts/build-source-navigation.mjs) from the [canonical source registry](../../data/registries/sources.yaml). [Machine-readable index](../../data/indexes/source-navigation.json). This is a derived navigation index; scientific records and the registry remain the owners.

## Coverage and limits

Authored Markdown and structured YAML/JSON only. Source snapshots, frozen archive, generated publication/build copies, quality reports, code and schema examples are excluded. Markdown code examples are excluded. A mention is navigation evidence, not claim validation. External destinations and heading anchors are not checked live.

The scan covers 2791 files, 410 registered sources, 410 sources mentioned in those files and 91858 resolved citation occurrences. A registered URL is a destination supplied by its owner; its presence does not prove accessibility or support for a scientific claim. Metadata not present in the registry remains unknown.

| Finding | Count | Action |
| --- | ---: | --- |
| Unresolved source identifiers | 44 | Review each owner; do not fabricate citations. |
| Sources without an external destination | 183 | Preserve local-only sources; add verified bibliographic destinations where available. |
| Sources without any known destination | 177 | Locate an original source or mark retrieval unresolved. |
| Sources without recorded retrieval metadata | 258 | Record retrieval scope/date during the next source review. |
| Sources without recorded licence metadata | 305 | Resolve redistribution rights before a publication release. |
| Missing registered local targets | 0 | Restore the documented snapshot or correct its registry path after review. |
| Missing internal document destinations | 0 | Correct the authored link or add its missing target. |
| Parse errors | 0 | Repair the input format before trusting coverage. |

## Source directory

Follow a source to its original destination or local snapshot, then follow its internal usage to the relevant record. The navigation index groups repeated references by owner file and preserves their counts. [Download every occurrence with its YAML/JSON field location](../../data/indexes/source-navigation-occurrences.json.gz) (compressed JSON).
